{"parentUuid":"9a93dd1d-e65b-4406-9ead-3c4ed4fefd89","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"8a61753f-1a68-41a9-b215-a727ff410d48","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:06:24.365Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"8a61753f-1a68-41a9-b215-a727ff410d48","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Codified as `sync-040` confirmed-aligned in `sync-state.md`. re_verify_when: wiki/ entity count exceeds 200 AND _index.md staleness > 1 week.\n\n### Q-D3 — Memory Tier Codification\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `memory-tiers.md` authoring (Track 7 amendment per REV-2) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster D\n- **question**: Codify Tier 1 (immutable always-loaded) / Tier 2 (just-in-time) / Tier 3 (explicit pull)?\n- **options**: A: Yes — add `memory-tiers.md` to constitution / B: Yes AND add hook to enforce / C: Already implicit in CLAUDE.md + autonomous-protocol — no new artifact / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Yes, add `memory-tiers.md` to constitution as formal artifact codifying always-loaded vs just-in-time vs explicit-pull tiers. To be drafted in `proposals/memory-tiers.md` during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E1 — Self-Detect-Drift Mechanism\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `self-application-bootstrap.md` authoring OR S8 Track 9 self-awareness implementation (whichever first) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: How agents auto-detect drift WITHOUT human prompt?\n- **options**: A: Per-task DA-rule (re-read spec every 5 steps) + checkpoint-vs-current diff hook / B: Mid-session `/session-verify` auto-fired by Stop hook every N tool calls / C: Fresh-context drift-auditor subagent dispatched mid-session at random/triggered intervals / D: All A+B+C / E: open answer\n- **answer**: D (Recommended) — All A+B+C combined; defense-in-depth matches stockforge multi-perspective principle. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Self-detect-drift mechanism during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E2 — Agent-Notes Promotion Frequency\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: How often does agent-notes → skill/hook/deterministic promotion run?\n- **options**: A: Phase-boundary only (manual review by promotion subagent, opus, fresh ctx) / B: Per-session-end Stop hook scans new agent-notes; dispatches subagent if ≥3 similar entries / C: Continuous — every new note triggers similarity check / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Phase-boundary only; manual review by promotion subagent. Cheapest; matches stockforge phase cadence. To be codified in `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § Promotion frequency during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E3 — Promotion Target Priority\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 (paired with E2) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: When agent-note becomes promotable, where does it land?\n- **options**: A: New skill in `.claude/skills/<name>/` / B: New hook script in `scripts/hooks/` / C: Update existing constitution file / D: All three valid; promotion subagent decides per pattern type — but priority MUST be hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST (cheapest first) / E: open answer\n- **answer**: D (Recommended; rephrased as \"Hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST\") — cheapest deterministic check first; skill encodes procedural discipline; charter amendment heaviest. To be codified in `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § Promotion target priority during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E4 — Auto-Detected-Drift Recovery Flow\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 (`autonomous-protocol.md` recovery section) OR S8 Track 9 (paired with E1) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: If drift auto-detected mid-session, recovery flow?\n- **options**: A: HALT immediately; emit ALERT; require human resume / B: HALT only if charter-tier; SOFT-WARN otherwise / C: Open Q&A bundle with detected drift + 3 remediation options; let human pick async / D: Auto-revert to last clean checkpoint + emit notification / E: open answer\n- **answer**: C (Recommended) — Open Q&A bundle with detected drift + 3 remediation options; let human pick async. Matches UP-06 NO-Silent-Default + mobile-remote use case. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Drift recovery flow during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-2.1 — Skill-Tool Autonomous Mode Policy\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `autonomous-protocol.md` authoring → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2, same turn)\n- **source**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-04-29-003-up05-askuser-permissions-rawsessions.md` cluster Q2\n- **question**: For autonomous run, how to handle Skill tool prompts (Anthropic-designed always-prompt)?\n- **options**: A: Avoid Skill calls in autonomous mode (replace with inline procedures + agent dispatch) / B: Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills / C: Batch Skill calls at session-start under explicit user approval (one-time per session) / D: Accept friction — pause on Skill prompt; not deal-breaker / E: open answer\n- **answer**: B (Recommended) — Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills (replace with inline procedures or subagent dispatch). Matches existing agent-notes UP-05 rule. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Skill-tool policy in autonomous mode during S16 IMPL.\n\n---\n\n## Closed (after answer)\n\n(none yet — append entries here as questions get fired + answered)\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Note for SessionStart Hook (Track 5, S3)\n\nHook script `scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh` (Track 5 deliverable) MUST include:\n\n```bash\n# Check queued-grill triggers\nQUEUED_GRILL_FILE=\"agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\"\nACTIVE_PHASE=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:/ {print $2}' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Active plan\\*\\*/' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\n\n# For each ### Q-* section, parse fire_when and check match\n# If match → emit notification: \"queued-grill ready: <Q-id>; consider firing AskUserQuestion in this session\"\n# Do NOT auto-fire — surface to agent who decides timing\n```\n\nThis keeps the queue auditable + actionable without LLM-Guardian continuous polling.\n","numLines":190,"startLine":1,"totalLines":190}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9f85e427-b8c8-431d-9597-919785c34364","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b172983f-f656-4186-9a79-073dd444f48f","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` (agent writes pending; human moves answered)\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` channel.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"47f5a007-b41d-43dc-aedf-153117bbf197","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:06:38.543Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

default-acceptance prohibited).\n31\t\n32\t## Why this rule exists\n33\t\n34\tThe self-upgrade loop (Karpathy outer-loop / decision-discipline § Rule 4) requires\n35\tthat lessons learned in real sessions feed back into KI-* / BP-* / agent-notes\n36\tentries, which then promote up to hooks / skills / charter via Rule 4 (5-session OR\n37\tphase-boundary). Empirically, Stage 2 (the *write-the-lesson-down* step itself) has\n38\tbeen the dormancy hotspot:\n39\t\n40\t- **S20 → S35**: 15 sessions elapsed before promote-rule fired (KI-S35-5).\n41\t- **S26 → S43b**: 9 sessions elapsed before lesson-synthesizer ran (KI-S43b-5).\n42\t- **L-S43b-7**: cataloged the failure mode — \"Stage 2 of Self-Upgrade Loop Has No Agent\".\n43\t\n44\tStage 1 (rule-discovery during work) is implicit in good engineering. Stage 2\n45\t(rule-recording) is the bottleneck. Without a deterministic rule forcing it,\n46\tsessions complete without synthesizing learnings, the corpus stagnates, and\n47\tpromote-rule cycles surface a thin set of stale candidates.\n48\t\n49\t## Proposed amendment text\n50\t\n51\tAppend to `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § Rule 4 region:\n52\t\n53\t```markdown\n54\t### Rule 4b — Lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end\n55\t\n56\tEvery session whose work produces ANY of the following triggers MUST append at\n57\tleast one new entry to `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md`\n58\tOR `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md` OR\n59\t`agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` BEFORE the checkpoint `latest.md` is\n60\twritten:\n61\t\n62\t  (a) ≥1 user correction (verbatim or paraphrased)\n63\t  (b) ≥1 deferred-fix item (R-N / DEFER-S*-N)\n64\t  (c) ≥1 substrate gap discovered (hook missing, contract unenforced, etc.)\n65\t  (d) ≥1 charter-tier or SCOPE-tier decision authored\n66\t  (e) ≥1 META_LOOP recovery action\n67\t\n68\tThe entry MUST cite at minimum: (i) verbatim trigger evidence; (ii) the rule it\n69\tcodifies; (iii) anti-example (what the rule prevents); (iv) auto-detect path\n70\t(hook / skill / charter). Sessions producing zero triggers (e.g., rote refactor\n71\tsessions) are exempt and SHOULD record `lesson_synthesis: NA-no-triggers` in\n72\ttheir session log.\n73\t\n74\t**Enforcement** (paired hook upgrade):\n75\t  - `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` is promoted from advisory\n76\t    (current default) to STRICT mode (exit 2 on dormancy detection).\n77\t  - Strict mode auto-clears once the next session writes a qualifying entry.\n78\t  - Loop is finite: ≤1 hard-block per dormancy episode.\n79\t\n80\t**Cross-references**: KI-S35-5, BP-S35-1, KI-S43b-5, BP-S43b-4, L-S43b-7.\n81\t**Supersedes**: nothing (additive to Rule 4).\n82\t**Companion**: Rule 4 promote-rule cadence remains 5-session OR phase-boundary;\n83\tthis rule shortens the *upstream* feedback latency that feeds Rule 4.\n84\t```\n85\t\n86\t## Why charter-tier (not skill, not just hook)\n87\t\n88\tPer Q-E3 promotion priority (closed S15) — hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST.\n89\tThis proposal lands at charter because:\n90\t\n91\t1. **The hook already exists** (`lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh`) but is advisory-only.\n92\t   Promoting it to strict-mode requires a charter-tier rule to cite as authority.\n93\t2. **The pattern reshapes self-upgrade-loop identity** — it codifies *that* the loop\n94\t   has a Stage 2 obligation, not merely *how* to enforce it. That's identity-level\n95\t   (per L-S15-1 charter-split rule).\n96\t3. **Skill insufficient** — this is a discipline rule, not a procedure. There is no\n97\t   sequence of LLM-judgment-required steps; the rule is \"write or be blocked\".\n98\t\n99\t## Trade-offs\n100\t\n101\t| Concern | Mitigation |\n102\t|---|---|\n103\t| **False-positive blocks on rote sessions** | Exemption clause `lesson_synthesis: NA-no-triggers` + 5 explicit trigger gates (a)-(e); rote refactor sessions self-attest exemption. |\n104\t| **Verbose KI/BP corpus growth** | Rule 4 promote-rule cadence (5-session OR phase-boundary) prunes via cluster-and-promote; growth pressure is already balanced by demote pressure. |\n105\t| **Strict-mode hard-block surprise** | Loop is finite (≤1 per episode); exit 2 surfaces stderr to assistant transcript per HR-7 pattern; agent writes entry → next Stop auto-clears. |\n106\t| **Watchdog itself dormant** | Hook is in `.claude/settings.json` Stop chain priority 12 (already deployed); strict-mode upgrade is a 1-line change in the script. |\n107\t\n108\t## Failure modes if NOT ratified\n109\t\n110\t- Recurrence: 6+ sessions of dormancy expected within next phase based on KI-S43b-5\n111\t  empirical baseline.\n112\t- Compounding: stale promote-rule candidate pool → low-quality cluster proposals →\n113\t  stale charter → meta-loop trust loss (per L-S43b-10 premature-stop family).\n114\t\n115\t## What ratification looks like (for the user)\n116\t\n117\tIf the user picks \"ACCEPT\":\n118\t1. scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh        # HR-4 routing-tree\n    - scripts/hooks/pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh     # HR-2 / HR-7\n---\n\n# Proposal: Decision-discipline Rule 4b — Lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end\n\n> **Status**: DRAFT — proposed for charter ratification at user discretion.\n> **Tier**: CHARTER (per L-S15-1: any rule reshaping self-upgrade-loop identity is charter-tier).\n> **Required action**: User explicit letter pick per Q-B2 closure (charter/SCOPE-tier MUST require explicit accept; default-acceptance prohibited).\n\n## Why this rule exists\n\nThe self-upgrade loop (Karpathy outer-loop / decision-discipline § Rule 4) requires\nthat lessons learned in real sessions feed back into KI-* / BP-* / agent-notes\nentries, which then promote up to hooks / skills / charter via Rule 4 (5-session OR\nphase-boundary). Empirically, Stage 2 (the *write-the-lesson-down* step itself) has\nbeen the dormancy hotspot:\n\n- **S20 → S35**: 15 sessions elapsed before promote-rule fired (KI-S35-5).\n- **S26 → S43b**: 9 sessions elapsed before lesson-synthesizer ran (KI-S43b-5).\n- **L-S43b-7**: cataloged the failure mode — \"Stage 2 of Self-Upgrade Loop Has No Agent\".\n\nStage 1 (rule-discovery during work) is implicit in good engineering. Stage 2\n(rule-recording) is the bottleneck. Without a deterministic rule forcing it,\nsessions complete without synthesizing learnings, the corpus stagnates, and\npromote-rule cycles surface a thin set of stale candidates.\n\n## Proposed amendment text\n\nAppend to `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § Rule 4 region:\n\n```markdown\n### Rule 4b — Lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end\n\nEvery session whose work produces ANY of the following triggers MUST append at\nleast one new entry to `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md`\nOR `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md` OR\n`agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` BEFORE the checkpoint `latest.md` is\nwritten:\n\n  (a) ≥1 user correction (verbatim or paraphrased)\n  (b) ≥1 deferred-fix item (R-N / DEFER-S*-N)\n  (c) ≥1 substrate gap discovered (hook missing, contract unenforced, etc.)\n  (d) ≥1 charter-tier or SCOPE-tier decision authored\n  (e) ≥1 META_LOOP recovery action\n\nThe entry MUST cite at minimum: (i) verbatim trigger evidence; (ii) the rule it\ncodifies; (iii) anti-example (what the rule prevents); (iv) auto-detect path\n(hook / skill / charter). Sessions producing zero triggers (e.g., rote refactor\nsessions) are exempt and SHOULD record `lesson_synthesis: NA-no-triggers` in\ntheir session log.\n\n**Enforcement** (paired hook upgrade):\n  - `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` is promoted from advisory\n    (current default) to STRICT mode (exit 2 on dormancy detection).\n  - Strict mode auto-clears once the next session writes a qualifying entry.\n  - Loop is finite: ≤1 hard-block per dormancy episode.\n\n**Cross-references**: KI-S35-5, BP-S35-1, KI-S43b-5, BP-S43b-4, L-S43b-7.\n**Supersedes**: nothing (additive to Rule 4).\n**Companion**: Rule 4 promote-rule cadence remains 5-session OR phase-boundary;\nthis rule shortens the *upstream* feedback latency that feeds Rule 4.\n```\n\n## Why charter-tier (not skill, not just hook)\n\nPer Q-E3 promotion priority (closed S15) — hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST.\nThis proposal lands at charter because:\n\n1. **The hook already exists** (`lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh`) but is advisory-only.\n   Promoting it to strict-mode requires a charter-tier rule to cite as authority.\n2. **The pattern reshapes self-upgrade-loop identity** — it codifies *that* the loop\n   has a Stage 2 obligation, not merely *how* to enforce it. That's identity-level\n   (per L-S15-1 charter-split rule).\n3. **Skill insufficient** — this is a discipline rule, not a procedure. There is no\n   sequence of LLM-judgment-required steps; the rule is \"write or be blocked\".\n\n## Trade-offs\n\n| Concern | Mitigation |\n|---|---|\n| **False-positive blocks on rote sessions** | Exemption clause `lesson_synthesis: NA-no-triggers` + 5 explicit trigger gates (a)-(e); rote refactor sessions self-attest exemption. |\n| **Verbose KI/BP corpus growth** | Rule 4 promote-rule cadence (5-session OR phase-boundary) prunes via cluster-and-promote; growth pressure is already balanced by demote pressure. |\n| **Strict-mode hard-block surprise** | Loop is finite (≤1 per episode); exit 2 surfaces stderr to assistant transcript per HR-7 pattern; agent writes entry → next Stop auto-clears. |\n| **Watchdog itself dormant** | Hook is in `.claude/settings.json` Stop chain priority 12 (already deployed); strict-mode upgrade is a 1-line change in the script. |\n\n## Failure modes if NOT ratified\n\n- Recurrence: 6+ sessions of dormancy expected within next phase based on KI-S43b-5\n  empirical baseline.\n- Compounding: stale promote-rule candidate pool → low-quality cluster proposals →\n  stale charter → meta-loop trust loss (per L-S43b-10 premature-stop family).\n\n## What ratification looks like (for the user)\n\nIf the user picks \"ACCEPT\":\n1. Agent moves this file to `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` via\n   the S38 deny-lift mechanism (charter-edit gate).\n2. Agent appends the amendment text verbatim under § Rule 4 region.\n3. Agent flips `lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` from advisory to strict (1-line edit:\n   `exit 0` → `exit 2` on dormancy branch).\n4. Agent authors `D-NNN-decision-discipline-rule-4b-ratified.md` ADR with\n   approval_chain pointing to the user pick.\n\nIf the user picks \"REJECT\":\n1. Mark this proposal `status: REJECTED` with reason.\n2. Continue advisory-mode watchdog indefinitely.\n3. Re-propose only if BP-S43b-4 violation recurs ≥3× within next phase.\n\nIf the user picks \"AMEND\":\n1. User specifies which clause to change.\n2. Agent revises this proposal; re-presents.\n\n## Related open proposals (sibling-cluster context)\n\n- `proposals/architecture-amendment.md` — adjacent self-upgrade-loop articulation.\n- `proposals/session-budgets-amendment.md` — sibling discipline rule on token budgets.\n\n## Ratification record\n\n(Empty until user pick lands.)\n","numLines":143,"startLine":1,"totalLines":143}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fc4487ce-c633-4fad-bcd6-f22f4788677e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Differs from feature/scope grill:\n4\t> questions verify shared understanding rather than propose new direction.\n5\t> Created S7 (2026-04-29) per Track 5.5b.4 (D-003 § 5.5b.4).\n6\t> Pairs with `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook (Track 5.5b.3).\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## When to use this template\n11\t\n12\tUse when ANY of:\n13\t- `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` emits SYNC-GRILLING DUE in additionalContext\n14\t- ≥3 items in `sync-state.md` have `state: assumed-aligned` for >7 days without re-confirmation\n15\t- ≥1 item in `sync-state.md` has `state: open-question` blocking active track\n16\t- Phase boundary about to fire (post-Phase-N close, pre-Phase-(N+1) entry)\n17\t\n18\tDo NOT use when:\n19\t- All sync-state items are `confirmed-aligned` and recent (<7 days)\n20\t- Active track requires immediate scope-tier or charter-tier decisions (use feature/scope grill instead)\n21\t\n22\t---\n23\t\n24\t## Composition rules (BINDING per UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n25\t\n26\t1. **Source items only from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`**. Do NOT invent new sync questions ad-hoc — those belong in feature/scope grill.\n27\t\n28\t2. **Phrase every question as alignment check**, not new direction. Patterns:\n29\t   - ✅ \"Do we still understand X the same way?\"\n30\t   - ✅ \"Has Y changed since we last confirmed it on {date}?\"\n31\t   - ✅ \"Is Z still the operational policy, or has practice diverged?\"\n32\t   - ❌ \"Should we change X?\" (this is feature/scope, not sync)\n33\t   - ❌ \"What direction for Y?\" (open-question, not alignment check)\n34\t\n35\t3. **Each question MUST cite exactly ONE sync-state item ID** (sync-NNN). Multi-item questions get split.\n36\t\n37\t4. **Multi-batch protocol**: max 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion` call. Bundle of N>4 sync items → ceil(N/4) batches across same session or sequential turns. NEVER use file-only-with-default-acceptance fallback (UP-06 hard rule).\n38\t\n39\t5. **Each option = single explicit pick**:\n40\t   - Option A: \"Yes, still aligned — confirmation re-stamp\"\n41\t   - Option B: \"No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain (other answer)\"\n42\t   - Option C (only when applicable): \"Partially — still aligned on X but Y has changed\"\n43\t   - NEVER use vague labels like \"I'm not sure\" or \"default applies after 24h\"\n44\t\n45\t6. **Closure protocol**: after answers received:\n46\t   - If A → update sync-state.md item `state: confirmed-aligned`, `confirmed_at: <today>`, `confirmation_via: AskUserQuestion sync-grill <date>`\n47\t   - If B → transition `state: open-question`, append `gap_note: <user explanation>`, queue follow-up via `queued-grill-master.md` with explicit `fire_when:` trigger\n48\t   - If C → split into two items; one confirmed, one open-question\n49\t\n50\t---\n51\t\n52\t## Bundle skeleton (use as-is, fill blanks)\n53\t\n54\t```markdown\n55\t## Sync Bundle — <YYYY-MM-DD> (S<N>)\n56\t\n57\t**Trigger**: <sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired | manual | phase-boundary>\n58\t**Last sync check**: <date from sync-state.md>\n59\t**Items checked this round** (N=<count>): sync-<id1>, sync-<id2>, ...\n60\t\n61\t### Q1 — sync-<NNN>: <statement from sync-state.md>\n62\t**Original confirmation date**: <date>\n63\t**Original basis**: <decision/AskUserQuestion ref>\n64\t\n65\tQuestion: \"Do we still understand <thing> the same way as <date>?\"\n66\tOptions:\n67\t- A: Yes, still aligned (confirmation re-stamp)\n68\t- B: No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain\n69\t- C (if applicable): Partially aligned — see notes\n70\t\n71\t### Q2 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n72\t[same format as Q1]\n73\t\n74\t### Q3 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n75\t[same format]\n76\t\n77\t### Q4 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n78\t[same format]\n79\t```\n80\t\n81\t---\n82\t\n83\t## Anti-patterns to avoid\n84\t\n85\t| Anti-pattern | Why bad | Correct |\n86\t|---|---|---|\n87\t| Mix sync + scope questions in same bundle | UP-06 charter-tier split rule (mixing dilutes alignment signal) | Separate bundles |\n88\t| Question without `sync-NNN` reference | sync-state.md becomes drift-prone | Always cite item ID |\n89\t| Ask 8+ sync questions in one batch | AskUserQuestion 4-limit + cognitive overload | Multi-batch |\n90\t| Use file-only \"answer by editing this file\" fallback | UP-06 silent-default rule | AskUserQuestion is PRIMARY |\n91\t| Omit `confirmation_via:` field on update | Lose audit trail | Always cite the AskUserQuestion turn |\n92\t\n93\t---\n94\t\n95\t## Worked example (4-question round, derived from sync-state.md current state)\n96\t\n97\t```\n98\t## Sync Bundle — 2026-05-10 (S10, post-Track 6 entry)\n99\t\n100\t**Trigger**: sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired (4 sessions since last check; threshold=3)\n101\t**Last sync check**: 2026-04-29\n102\t**Items checked this round** (N=4): sync-002, sync-008, sync-015, sync-021\n103\t\n104\t### Q1 — sync-002: \"Phase 0 = Harness Bootstrap, supervised until Track 7\"\n105\t**Original confirmation date**: 2026-04-29 (D-003 Round 1+2)\n106\tQuestion: \"Do we still understand Phase 0 as supervised-until-Track-7, or has the autonomous\n107\tboundary shifted given S6-S9 progress?\"\n108\tOptions:\n109\t- A: Yes, supervised through Track 7 (S11) as confirmed\n110\t- B: No, ready to flip autonomous earlier — explain when\n111\t- C: Partially — autonomous for {scope} but supervised for {scope}\n112\t\n113\t### Q2 — sync-008: \"...\"\n114\t[same format]\n115\t```\n116\t\n117\t---\n118\t\n119\t## Wiring with grill-maximization skill\n120\t\n121\tAdd this section to `.claude/skills/grill-maximization/SKILL.md` (manual edit per Track 6 progressive-disclosure refactor):\n122\t\n123\t```markdown\n124\t## Sync Bundle Composition (Track 5.5b.4)\n125\t\n126\tFor periodic alignment rounds, use `references/sync-bundle-template.md`:\n127\t- Trigger: `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook\n128\t- Source: items from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`\n129\t- Phrasing: alignment checks, not new directions\n130\t- Limit: 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion`; multi-batch for N>4\n131\t- Closure: update sync-state.md `state` + `confirmed_at` after answers\n132\t```\n133\t"}]},"uuid":"d64cebf4-0691-4ce0-8c1e-893620ad12e5","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:07:04.625Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\grill-maximization\\references\\sync-bundle-template.md","content":"# Sync-Bundle Template — Specialized Q&A for \"Are We Still Aligned?\"\n\n> Specialized Q&A template for periodic sync-grilling rounds. Differs from feature/scope grill:\n> questions verify shared understanding rather than propose new direction.\n> Created S7 (2026-04-29) per Track 5.5b.4 (D-003 § 5.5b.4).\n> Pairs with `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook (Track 5.5b.3).\n\n---\n\n## When to use this template\n\nUse when ANY of:\n- `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` emits SYNC-GRILLING DUE in additionalContext\n- ≥3 items in `sync-state.md` have `state: assumed-aligned` for >7 days without re-confirmation\n- ≥1 item in `sync-state.md` has `state: open-question` blocking active track\n- Phase boundary about to fire (post-Phase-N close, pre-Phase-(N+1) entry)\n\nDo NOT use when:\n- All sync-state items are `confirmed-aligned` and recent (<7 days)\n- Active track requires immediate scope-tier or charter-tier decisions (use feature/scope grill instead)\n\n---\n\n## Composition rules (BINDING per UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n\n1. **Source items only from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`**. Do NOT invent new sync questions ad-hoc — those belong in feature/scope grill.\n\n2. **Phrase every question as alignment check**, not new direction. Patterns:\n   - ✅ \"Do we still understand X the same way?\"\n   - ✅ \"Has Y changed since we last confirmed it on {date}?\"\n   - ✅ \"Is Z still the operational policy, or has practice diverged?\"\n   - ❌ \"Should we change X?\" (this is feature/scope, not sync)\n   - ❌ \"What direction for Y?\" (open-question, not alignment check)\n\n3. **Each question MUST cite exactly ONE sync-state item ID** (sync-NNN). Multi-item questions get split.\n\n4. **Multi-batch protocol**: max 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion` call. Bundle of N>4 sync items → ceil(N/4) batches across same session or sequential turns. NEVER use file-only-with-default-acceptance fallback (UP-06 hard rule).\n\n5. **Each option = single explicit pick**:\n   - Option A: \"Yes, still aligned — confirmation re-stamp\"\n   - Option B: \"No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain (other answer)\"\n   - Option C (only when applicable): \"Partially — still aligned on X but Y has changed\"\n   - NEVER use vague labels like \"I'm not sure\" or \"default applies after 24h\"\n\n6. **Closure protocol**: after answers received:\n   - If A → update sync-state.md item `state: confirmed-aligned`, `confirmed_at: <today>`, `confirmation_via: AskUserQuestion sync-grill <date>`\n   - If B → transition `state: open-question`, append `gap_note: <user explanation>`, queue follow-up via `queued-grill-master.md` with explicit `fire_when:` trigger\n   - If C → split into two items; one confirmed, one open-question\n\n---\n\n## Bundle skeleton (use as-is, fill blanks)\n\n```markdown\n## Sync Bundle — <YYYY-MM-DD> (S<N>)\n\n**Trigger**: <sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired | manual | phase-boundary>\n**Last sync check**: <date from sync-state.md>\n**Items checked this round** (N=<count>): sync-<id1>, sync-<id2>, ...\n\n### Q1 — sync-<NNN>: <statement from sync-state.md>\n**Original confirmation date**: <date>\n**Original basis**: <decision/AskUserQuestion ref>\n\nQuestion: \"Do we still understand <thing> the same way as <date>?\"\nOptions:\n- A: Yes, still aligned (confirmation re-stamp)\n- B: No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain\n- C (if applicable): Partially aligned — see notes\n\n### Q2 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n[same format as Q1]\n\n### Q3 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n[same format]\n\n### Q4 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n[same format]\n```\n\n---\n\n## Anti-patterns to avoid\n\n| Anti-pattern | Why bad | Correct |\n|---|---|---|\n| Mix sync + scope questions in same bundle | UP-06 charter-tier split rule (mixing dilutes alignment signal) | Separate bundles |\n| Question without `sync-NNN` reference | sync-state.md becomes drift-prone | Always cite item ID |\n| Ask 8+ sync questions in one batch | AskUserQuestion 4-limit + cognitive overload | Multi-batch |\n| Use file-only \"answer by editing this file\" fallback | UP-06 silent-default rule | AskUserQuestion is PRIMARY |\n| Omit `confirmation_via:` field on update | Lose audit trail | Always cite the AskUserQuestion turn |\n\n---\n\n## Worked example (4-question round, derived from sync-state.md current state)\n\n```\n## Sync Bundle — 2026-05-10 (S10, post-Track 6 entry)\n\n**Trigger**: sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired (4 sessions since last check; threshold=3)\n**Last sync check**: 2026-04-29\n**Items checked this round** (N=4): sync-002, sync-008, sync-015, sync-021\n\n### Q1 — sync-002: \"Phase 0 = Harness Bootstrap, supervised until Track 7\"\n**Original confirmation date**: 2026-04-29 (D-003 Round 1+2)\nQuestion: \"Do we still understand Phase 0 as supervised-until-Track-7, or has the autonomous\nboundary shifted given S6-S9 progress?\"\nOptions:\n- A: Yes, supervised through Track 7 (S11) as confirmed\n- B: No, ready to flip autonomous earlier — explain when\n- C: Partially — autonomous for {scope} but supervised for {scope}\n\n### Q2 — sync-008: \"...\"\n[same format]\n```\n\n---\n\n## Wiring with grill-maximization skill\n\nAdd this section to `.claude/skills/grill-maximization/SKILL.md` (manual edit per Track 6 progressive-disclosure refactor):\n\n```markdown\n## Sync Bundle Composition (Track 5.5b.4)\n\nFor periodic alignment rounds, use `references/sync-bundle-template.md`:\n- Trigger: `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook\n- Source: items from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`\n- Phrasing: alignment checks, not new directions\n- Limit: 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion`; multi-batch for N>4\n- Closure: update sync-state.md `state` + `confirmed_at` after answers\n```\n","numLines":133,"startLine":1,"totalLines":133}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d3d294dc-0d7f-43f8-9e0a-6fd3283306ca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

≤250K (MULTI_TASK_IMPL); ≤80K (PLAN); ≤150K (whole-Phase VERIFY per L-S21-1 calibrated)\n> - Never mix PLAN + IMPL in same session\n> - Sandwich pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier) for non-trivial work; Phase 2 close = sandwich-verifier whole-Phase review per S29 model\n> - NO LLM math; numbers from code only (Charter Principle 9; I-S1)\n> - Every claim has source + as-of date (Charter Principle 1; I-1; I-S5)\n> - Constitution edits route through `agent-workspace/proposals/` first; user explicit approve required (Track E)\n> - Domain layer = pure stdlib + dataclasses + Enum (zero framework deps); Pydantic only in interfaces/infrastructure\n> - Cross-BC communication via `packages/contracts/` only (per IMPL-S27-1 + IMPL-S27-2 doctrine)\n> - Agents never `git commit` unless user explicitly requests\n> - Track E (proposals batch) is fully PARALLEL — does not block IMPL sessions per D-011 § Option C analysis\n> - Track A (R2 closure) is BLOCKER for Track B VN30 rollout — Rule 4 multi-source reconciliation requires ≥2 working providers for ≥80% of VN30\n> - VBW pre-flight per L-S30-1 doctrine: every proposed deliverable file path must be `ls`-verified NEW or EDIT-ratify\n\n---\n\n## Charter Alignment\n\nPhase 2 closes Charter Month-3 deferrals (Tier 1+2 VN30 + 50 dossiers + 5 thesis) and partially advances Charter Month-6 (Tier 1+2 ingestion for top 100 — Phase 2 scope = top 30 VN30 only; top 100 deferred to Phase 3 entry tail).\n\n| # | Charter Criterion | Charter Phase | Phase 2 mapping (this plan) |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Tier 1 + 2 data pipeline operational for VN30 | Month 3 | DONE end-S43 — BC-1 VN30 ingestion (Track B) + BC-2 Fundamental (Track C) + BC-5 News stub (Track D) operational on all 30 VN30 tickers |\n| 2 | 50 companies have basic dossier in wiki | Month 3 | PARTIAL — VN30 = 30 dossiers (not 50); 20 mid-cap dossiers deferred to Phase 3 entry. Honest framing: spec 000 § A.4 already deferred Charter \"50\" → Phase 2; Phase 2 ships 30; remaining 20 = Phase 3 entry first sprint. |\n| 3 | First 5 thesis recorded with formal structure | Month 3 | DONE end-S43 — Track F produces ≥5 REAL thesis (`real_thesis: true`) on VN30 stocks via working `/thesis-validate` pipeline; per Q-S30-1 doctrine VHM-exemplar (`real_thesis: false`) does NOT count |\n| 4 | Can run `/thesis-validate` on a stock end-to-end in <5 minutes | Month 3 | DONE end-S43 — full automation via Track F (real `BearPerspectiveAgent` + `BullPerspectiveAgent` + `QuantPerspectiveAgent` + `Phase1Synthesizer` + Streamlit page); per spec 001 § A.3 AC-1 + AC-5 reproducibility + cost ceiling $3 |\n| 5 | 30+ KOL channels tracked | Month 6 | OUT-OF-SCOPE Phase 2 — KOL = Tier 3 = Phase 3 (re-numbered original Charter Phase 2 Edge Sources). Spec 002 not exercised. |\n| 6 | Tier 3 + 4 ingestion operational top 100 | Month 6 | OUT-OF-SCOPE Phase 2. |\n| 7 | First detectable pump pattern caught | Month 6 | OUT-OF-SCOPE Phase 2. |\n\n**Phase numbering note**: D-011 SCOPE-tier explicitly framed Phase 2 = \"Tier 1+2 VN30 rollout\" — this consolidates Charter Month-3 (originally Phase 1) + partial Month-6 (originally Phase 2). Original Charter \"Phase 2 Edge Sources\" Tier 3+4 = re-numbered to Phase 3 in the execution sequence. Phase-numbering audit recommended at Phase 2 close session (S43 + lessons-learned ceremony) — propose either (a) ratify D-011 re-numbering as durable (charter immutable; revision protocol triggered) OR (b) keep D-011 re-numbering executional-only and treat charter phase IDs as historical reference. Recommend (b) — charter immutability is sacred per Revision Protocol; D-011 lives in current-execution.md and decisions/ as the runtime authority.\n\n---\n\n## Track Catalog (A-F)\n\n### Track A — R2 Closure (BLOCKER for Track B)\n\n**Goal**: Establish ≥2 working bar-source providers for ≥80% of VN30 tickers so Rule 4 multi-source reconciliation has signal. R2 (S29 verifier residue) = TCBS public API returned 404 for VHM ingestion → SINGLE_SOURCE=248 only. Cannot ship VN30 with all 30 tickers single-source per Rule 4 + financial-data-protocol-amendment-VN.md Rule 14 sàn-tier discipline.\n\n**Strategy options** (S32 PLAN session evaluates + picks):\n- **A1**: Discover working TCBS endpoint (path/query/header probe; vendor-API archaeology)\n- **A2**: Pivot to DNSE or KBS via `vnstock` 4.0.2 alternate sources (vnstock has multiple source backends per IMPL-S28-1)\n- **A3**: SSI / VnDirect public API direct (skip vnstock wrapper; httpx adapter à la TcbsAdapter)\n- **A4**: Accept SCOPE-tier deviation — document SINGLE_SOURCE-only Phase 2 with explicit Rule 4 waiver via proposal; rely on vnstock-only with per-ticker freshness sanity checks\n\n**Recommended A2 first** (vnstock 4.0.2 supports VCI / TCBS / SSI / DNSE backends; lowest engineering cost; preserves existing adapter Protocol). Fall to A3 if vnstock alternate-source coverage <80% VN30. A4 = last resort (requires user SCOPE-tier approve).\n\n**Dependencies**: NONE (Track A unblocks B).\n**Estimated session count**: 1 (S32 — FOCUSED_IMPL, ~120-140K).\n**Budget envelope**: ~120-140K.\n**Failure mode**: If A1+A2+A3 all <80% VN30 coverage, S32 escalates to A4 SCOPE-tier user-gate before exiting.\n\n### Track B — VN30 Universe Expansion (BC-1 ingestion)\n\n**Goal**: Scale BC-1 ingestion from 1 ticker (VHM thin-slice) to all 30 VN30 tickers; reuse existing `vnstock_adapter.py` + `tcbs_adapter.py` (or A2/A3 substitutes from Track A); generalize CLI `apps/cli/ingest_vhm.py` to `apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py` (NEW); 1-year backfill window per Q1 query pattern in VHM exemplar; reconciliation per Rule 4 + per-Sàn tolerance per amendment-VN Rule 14.\n\n**Key design choice**: Streaming ingestion vs nightly batch — pick **nightly batch** for Phase 2 (simpler; matches spec-T1-001 § B.7 cadence \"tier_1_hard_data: eod: 16:00 daily\"; streaming = Phase 3 when intraday lands). 30 tickers × 250 days ≈ 7,500 bars per source × 2 sources = ~15K rows; SQLite handles this comfortably (TimescaleDB still deferred per IMPL-S28 doctrine).\n\n**Sub-deliverables**:","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":791}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"35031c92-faf7-4153-aa75-3ce64c12dc2f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

do\n28\t  if grep -qE \"(import $LIB|from $LIB|$LIB\\.)\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ACTIVE_PLAN\"; then\n29\t    # Probe library importability + version.\n30\t    PROBE=$(python -c \"\n31\timport importlib, sys\n32\ttry:\n33\t    m = importlib.import_module('$LIB')\n34\t    v = getattr(m, '__version__', 'unknown')\n35\t    print(f'OK $LIB v={v}')\n36\texcept Exception as e:\n37\t    print(f'FAIL $LIB err={e}')\n38\t    sys.exit(1)\n39\t\" 2>&1) || true\n40\t    if echo \"$PROBE\" | grep -q '^FAIL'; probe each;\n# emit drift-log if API surface differs from master-plan-claimed.\n#\n# Wire as SessionStart hook AFTER Phase 0 close (S22+).\nset -euo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n[ ! -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Active session-plan path resolution.\nACTIVE_PLAN=$(grep -oE 'session-plans/pending/[0-9-]+[^ )\"`]+\\.md' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | head -1 || true)\n[ -z \"$ACTIVE_PLAN\" ] && exit 0\n[ ! -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ACTIVE_PLAN\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Whitelist of vendor libraries we care about (extend as project grows).\nVENDOR_LIBS=\"vnstock yfinance httpx anthropic openai duckdb pgvector\"\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nfor LIB in $VENDOR_LIBS; do\n  if grep -qE \"(import $LIB|from $LIB|$LIB\\.)\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ACTIVE_PLAN\"; then\n    # Probe library importability + version.\n    PROBE=$(python -c \"\nimport importlib, sys\ntry:\n    m = importlib.import_module('$LIB')\n    v = getattr(m, '__version__', 'unknown')\n    print(f'OK $LIB v={v}')\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f'FAIL $LIB err={e}')\n    sys.exit(1)\n\" 2>&1) || true\n    if echo \"$PROBE\" | grep -q '^FAIL'; then\n      VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n      echo \"vendor-api-probe FAIL: $PROBE (referenced in $ACTIVE_PLAN)\" >&2\n    fi\n    printf '[%s] probe %s : %s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$LIB\" \"$PROBE\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# Multi-strategy ladder detection (L-S32-1).\nLADDER=$(grep -cE 'Strategy A[1-4]|alternative [A-D]:|ladder' \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ACTIVE_PLAN\" || echo 0)\nif [ \"$LADDER\" -ge 3 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] multi-strategy ladder detected (count=%s) in %s — probe-first doctrine recommended\\n' \\\n    \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$LADDER\" \"$ACTIVE_PLAN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"vendor-api-probe INFO: multi-strategy ladder detected (≥3 strategies); {"parentUuid":"7f04d921-a3bf-4769-b06c-639d2e6a8fd4","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0f113386-2d0d-42ba-8b6d-aeb67bfc8ad9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QB1XzqDu8iz1m2B3mcqJ6h","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: decompose-work\n3\tdescription: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n4\tallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash]\n5\t---\n6\t\n7\t# Skill: decompose-work\n8\t\n9\t## Purpose\n10\t\n11\tKarpathy autoresearch loop foundation: BEFORE doing a task, **classify sub-parts as deterministic (cheap, cacheable, verifiable) vs LLM-required (judgment, ambiguous, generative)**. Mechanical work goes to scripts; LLM cycles go to where judgment is irreducible. Avoids the two failure modes: (a) burning LLM tokens on `wc -l` style work that bash does for free, (b) hand-coding logic that should be a one-paragraph judgment call.\n12\t\n13\tPer UP-06 §3 verbatim: *\"cần phải có một skill hoặc agent với khả năng chuyên biệt giúp nó 'bóc tách' được ra thế nào là 'deterministic' và thế nào là 'llm probability'\"*.\n14\t\n15\t## When to Use\n16\t\n17\t- Task has ≥3 distinct sub-parts\n18\t- Some sub-parts smell mechanical, others smell judgment-heavy\n19\t- Estimated context cost ≥30K if done naively LLM-only\n20\t- About to dispatch subagent — knowing the deterministic floor saves token budget\n21\t- Authoring a new skill or hook — decomposition surfaces what belongs in the script vs prompt\n22\t\n23\t## Pre-Flight Checklist (per L-S30-1; APPLIED 4× S30/S31/S33/S34/S35)\n24\t\n25\tBefore authoring any deliverable list (PLAN session) OR before writing files claimed by a master-plan (IMPL session), run VBW pre-flight:\n26\t\n27\t1. **Glob the claimed paths** — every path mentioned in deliverables; surface mismatches.\n28\t2. **Read the file** if expected to be modified; do not trust stale master-plan claims about file contents.\n29\t3. **`ls` target directories** — surface starter-kit files (e.g., SEED.md, _template.md) that may already satisfy deliverable purpose.\n30\t4. **Document mismatch** as IMPL-S{N}-* deviation rather than fabricating; absorb-or-replace decision is IMPL-tier per L-S11-2.\n31\t\n32\tThis is NOT optional for PLAN-tier deliverables; master-plans accumulate path-drift between authoring and IMPL. S30 caught `eval-sets/labeled-pumps/seed.md` nonexistent → ratified existing SEED.md instead. S33 caught `value_objects/` directory absence vs master-plan stale claim. S31 master-planner subagent applied this protocol successfully for 9 proposals + `apps/dashboard/` absence verification. The skill is binding when L-S30-1 applies.\n33\t\n34\t## When NOT to Use\n35\t\n36\t- Task is trivial (≤2 steps, single tool call)\n37\t- Task is purely deterministic (just run the script directly)\n38\t- Task is purely judgment-only with no measurable outputs (e.g., \"draft this paragraph\" — LLM end-to-end)\n39\t- Already mid-execution and re-decomposing would just be ceremonious\n40\t\n41\t## Inputs\n42\t\n43\t| Arg | Required | Purpose |\n44\t|---|---|---|\n45\t| `task_description` | yes | Free-form text describing the goal |\n46\t| `--budget` | no | Approximate token budget (helps prioritize deterministic offload) |\n47\t| `--depth` | no | `shallow` (3-5 portions) \\| `deep` (full sub-tree). Default shallow. |\n48\t\n49\t## Process\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"4cd1c060-ceed-47d1-b2a5-cb29b2283418","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:12:18.495Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\decompose-work\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: decompose-work\ndescription: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\nallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash]\n---\n\n# Skill: decompose-work\n\n## Purpose\n\nKarpathy autoresearch loop foundation: BEFORE doing a task, **classify sub-parts as deterministic (cheap, cacheable, verifiable) vs LLM-required (judgment, ambiguous, generative)**. Mechanical work goes to scripts; LLM cycles go to where judgment is irreducible. Avoids the two failure modes: (a) burning LLM tokens on `wc -l` style work that bash does for free, (b) hand-coding logic that should be a one-paragraph judgment call.\n\nPer UP-06 §3 verbatim: *\"cần phải có một skill hoặc agent với khả năng chuyên biệt giúp nó 'bóc tách' được ra thế nào là 'deterministic' và thế nào là 'llm probability'\"*.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Task has ≥3 distinct sub-parts\n- Some sub-parts smell mechanical, others smell judgment-heavy\n- Estimated context cost ≥30K if done naively LLM-only\n- About to dispatch subagent — knowing the deterministic floor saves token budget\n- Authoring a new skill or hook — decomposition surfaces what belongs in the script vs prompt\n\n## Pre-Flight Checklist (per L-S30-1; APPLIED 4× S30/S31/S33/S34/S35)\n\nBefore authoring any deliverable list (PLAN session) OR before writing files claimed by a master-plan (IMPL session), run VBW pre-flight:\n\n1. **Glob the claimed paths** — every path mentioned in deliverables; surface mismatches.\n2. **Read the file** if expected to be modified; do not trust stale master-plan claims about file contents.\n3. **`ls` target directories** — surface starter-kit files (e.g., SEED.md, _template.md) that may already satisfy deliverable purpose.\n4. **Document mismatch** as IMPL-S{N}-* deviation rather than fabricating; absorb-or-replace decision is IMPL-tier per L-S11-2.\n\nThis is NOT optional for PLAN-tier deliverables; master-plans accumulate path-drift between authoring and IMPL. S30 caught `eval-sets/labeled-pumps/seed.md` nonexistent → ratified existing SEED.md instead. S33 caught `value_objects/` directory absence vs master-plan stale claim. S31 master-planner subagent applied this protocol successfully for 9 proposals + `apps/dashboard/` absence verification. The skill is binding when L-S30-1 applies.\n\n## When NOT to Use\n\n- Task is trivial (≤2 steps, single tool call)\n- Task is purely deterministic (just run the script directly)\n- Task is purely judgment-only with no measurable outputs (e.g., \"draft this paragraph\" — LLM end-to-end)\n- Already mid-execution and re-decomposing would just be ceremonious\n\n## Inputs\n\n| Arg | Required | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| `task_description` | yes | Free-form text describing the goal |\n| `--budget` | no | Approximate token budget (helps prioritize deterministic offload) |\n| `--depth` | no | `shallow` (3-5 portions) \\| `deep` (full sub-tree). Default shallow. |\n\n## Process\n","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":114}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7f04d921-a3bf-4769-b06c-639d2e6a8fd4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"fc4f573f-76b9-4e21-8c10-d24a2196c337","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Md9rDj4sukFnf3MxLUyP5T","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GRbpYq3uBUBaiF1mhnGkCR","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\empirical-probe-first\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: empirical-probe-first\ndescription: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\nallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash, Write]\n---\n\n# Skill: empirical-probe-first\n\n## Purpose\n\nWhen a master-plan / ADR / spec lists multiple competing strategies and recommends one, **probe ALL viable options empirically before commit** rather than trusting the recommendation. The recommendation's source-evidence (vendor docs, blog posts, prior session notes) may be stale, especially for vendor APIs that drift silently between releases.\n\nSurfaced at S32 (Track A R2 closure):\n- Master-plan 005 § Track A recommended A2 (vnstock 4.0.2 alternate-source backends).\n- Empirical probe revealed A1 (TCBS endpoints) all 404 + A2 (vnstock Quote effectively VCI-only — TCBS/DNSE/SSI/FMARKET deprecated as Quote sources, MSN ConnectionError).\n- A3 (SSI iBoard direct httpx adapter) — not initially recommended — was the only viable path. Adopted; R2 CLOSED with 100% VN30 coverage.\n\nWithout empirical probe, the session would have committed to A2 and shipped a degraded SINGLE_SOURCE pipeline.\n\nThis skill encodes the procedural discipline; companion deterministic detection lives in `scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh` (SessionStart hook; advisory stderr when ladder count ≥3 detected in active plan).\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Active session-plan / ADR / spec lists ≥3 strategy options for ONE problem (Strategy A1/A2/A3, alternative B/C/D, ladder etc.)\n- Master-plan recommendation involves vendor API or library version that has shipped ≥1 release since the recommendation was authored\n- Predecessor session relied on the same library and recorded surface-area assumptions (extends L-S28-1 vendor-API drift family)\n- About to author production adapter / integration code and the choice is reversible only at high cost\n- `vendor-api-probe.sh` SessionStart hook emitted the \"multi-strategy ladder detected\" advisory\n\n## When NOT to Use\n\n- Only one strategy proposed (no ladder)\n- Strategy is pure-stdlib (no vendor surface to drift)\n- Probe cost > likely savings (e.g., 5-min probe to dodge 30-second commit cost)\n- Task already mid-IMPL and rolling back probe-then-pick would cost more than committing\n\n## Process\n\n1. **Enumerate** — list every strategy option from the source artifact verbatim. Quote the recommendation phrase.\n2. **Pre-flight VBW** (per L-S30-1) — `Glob` / `Read` every file the recommendation claims exists.\n3. **Per-strategy probe** — for each option run minimal viability check:\n   - Importability (`python -c \"import X; print(X.__version__)\"`)\n   - Surface-area sample call against ONE representative input (e.g., 1 ticker × 5 days for a market-data adapter)\n   - Capture exit code + stderr + first 200 chars of stdout\n4. **Build probe matrix** — write `data/<track>-probe-<vendor>.json` with one row per strategy:\n   ```json\n   {\"strategy\": \"A2\", \"verdict\": \"REJECT\", \"evidence\": \"vnstock.Quote returns VCI-only post-4.0.2; TCBS/DNSE/SSI/FMARKET deprecated\", \"probed_at\": \"2026-04-30T14:22:00+07:00\"}\n   ```\n5. **Pick** — first strategy with verdict=PASS; if multiple PASS, prefer simplest. If all REJECT → escalate to SCOPE-tier user-gate (do not silently degrade).\n6. **Document deviation** — if pick differs from master-plan recommendation, author IMPL-tier ADR (`agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-...md`) with `source_evidence` citing the probe matrix path. State explicitly: \"master-plan recommended X; empirical probe rejected X; adopted Y per probe matrix\".\n7. **Update lesson log** — if probe revealed stale source-evidence, append to `agent-notes.md` under \"Vendor API drift family (L-S28-1 / L-S32-1)\" cluster.\n\n## Outputs\n\n| Artifact | Path | Required? |\n|---|---|---|\n| Probe matrix (machine-readable) | `data/<track>-probe-<vendor>.json` | Yes |\n| Coverage report (human-readable) | `data/<track>-coverage-S{N}.md` | Yes if ladder picked involves vendor |\n| IMPL-tier ADR | `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-...md` | Yes if pick differs from recommendation |\n| SCOPE-tier escalation | AskUserQuestion (charter-tier split rule applies) | Only if all options REJECT |\n\n## Pre-Flight Checklist (binding when applies)\n\n- [ ] Active session-plan path resolved via `current-execution.md` (not hardcoded from memory)\n- [ ] Strategy enumeration is verbatim from source — no agent paraphrase\n- [ ] Each probe captured stderr (not just stdout) — silent failures are common with vendor SDKs\n- [ ] Probe matrix written BEFORE pick decision — order matters for audit trail\n- [ ] If pick differs from recommendation, IMPL-tier ADR cites probe matrix path in `source_evidence`\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n| Anti-pattern | Why bad | Correction |\n|---|---|---|\n| Trust the master-plan recommendation without probe | Vendor APIs drift silently; predecessor session's evidence may be stale | Always probe when ladder ≥3 |\n| Probe only the recommended option | Defeats the purpose; can't compare | Probe ALL viable options before pick |\n| Probe but pick recommendation anyway \"to honor the plan\" | Calibration drift; loses the empirical signal | Document the deviation as IMPL-tier ADR |\n| Silently degrade to last-viable when all REJECT | Hides scope reduction from human; violates UP-06 NO-Silent-Default | Escalate via AskUserQuestion SCOPE-tier |\n| Probe matrix fields opaque | Future audit cannot reconstruct decision | Use `{strategy, verdict, evidence, probed_at}` schema |\n\n## Pairs With\n\n- `scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh` — deterministic SessionStart hook detects ladder + emits advisory\n- `decompose-work` skill — splits each probe into deterministic (importability check) vs LLM (verdict synthesis)\n- `try-n-approaches` skill — Karpathy outer-loop framing; this skill is the strategy-pick lens within an approach\n- `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/012-track-A-source-pivot.md` — exemplar ADR following this protocol (S32 origin)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/013-S35-meta-loop-recovery-promote-routing.md` — S35 promote-routing decision that surfaced this skill as the next-tier promotion target\n\n## Provenance\n\n- **Origin**: L-S32-1 (\"Empirical probe before strategy commit\"; surfaced 2026-04-30 at S32 Track A R2 closure)\n- **Hook companion shipped**: S35 META_LOOP recovery (`scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh`)\n- **Skill promotion** (this file): Phase 2 close ceremony S43e (continuation 3); Phase 3 prereq #3 from `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/2026-05-04-phase-2-retrospective.md`\n- **Per Q-E3 promotion priority**: hook FIRST (DONE S35) → skill SECOND (THIS) → charter LAST (deferred unless ≥3 violations recur Phase 3)\n- **Cluster siblings**: L-S28-1 vendor-API drift family (vnstock 4.0.2 surface change); extends to KOL/news scrapers in Phase 3 (BeautifulSoup selector drift, RSS schema drift, etc.)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2099,"cache_read_input_tokens":125394,"output_tokens":3434,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2099,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3434,"cache_read_input_tokens":125394,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2099,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2099},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CahkBDHtGjiis2WSY4qPf","type":"assistant","uuid":"a2183e0a-a5a5-44f6-9a9c-5088003a779a","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:13:19.086Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
