{"parentUuid":"a2183e0a-a5a5-44f6-9a9c-5088003a779a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0f113386-2d0d-42ba-8b6d-aeb67bfc8ad9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GRbpYq3uBUBaiF1mhnGkCR","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\empirical-probe-first\\SKILL.md"}]},"uuid":"a22fbcdb-57b5-41dc-aa15-87c97c5b1baa","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:13:19.521Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\empirical-probe-first\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: empirical-probe-first\ndescription: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\nallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash, Write]\n---\n\n# Skill: empirical-probe-first\n\n## Purpose\n\nWhen a master-plan / ADR / spec lists multiple competing strategies and recommends one, **probe ALL viable options empirically before commit** rather than trusting the recommendation. The recommendation's source-evidence (vendor docs, blog posts, prior session notes) may be stale, especially for vendor APIs that drift silently between releases.\n\nSurfaced at S32 (Track A R2 closure):\n- Master-plan 005 § Track A recommended A2 (vnstock 4.0.2 alternate-source backends).\n- Empirical probe revealed A1 (TCBS endpoints) all 404 + A2 (vnstock Quote effectively VCI-only — TCBS/DNSE/SSI/FMARKET deprecated as Quote sources, MSN ConnectionError).\n- A3 (SSI iBoard direct httpx adapter) — not initially recommended — was the only viable path. Adopted; R2 CLOSED with 100% VN30 coverage.\n\nWithout empirical probe, the session would have committed to A2 and shipped a degraded SINGLE_SOURCE pipeline.\n\nThis skill encodes the procedural discipline; companion deterministic detection lives in `scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh` (SessionStart hook; advisory stderr when ladder count ≥3 detected in active plan).\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Active session-plan / ADR / spec lists ≥3 strategy options for ONE problem (Strategy A1/A2/A3, alternative B/C/D, ladder etc.)\n- Master-plan recommendation involves vendor API or library version that has shipped ≥1 release since the recommendation was authored\n- Predecessor session relied on the same library and recorded surface-area assumptions (extends L-S28-1 vendor-API drift family)\n- About to author production adapter / integration code and the choice is reversible only at high cost\n- `vendor-api-probe.sh` SessionStart hook emitted the \"multi-strategy ladder detected\" advisory\n\n## When NOT to Use\n\n- Only one strategy proposed (no ladder)\n- Strategy is pure-stdlib (no vendor surface to drift)\n- Probe cost > likely savings (e.g., 5-min probe to dodge 30-second commit cost)\n- Task already mid-IMPL and rolling back probe-then-pick would cost more than committing\n\n## Process\n\n1. **Enumerate** — list every strategy option from the source artifact verbatim. Quote the recommendation phrase.\n2. **Pre-flight VBW** (per L-S30-1) — `Glob` / `Read` every file the recommendation claims exists.\n3. **Per-strategy probe** — for each option run minimal viability check:\n   - Importability (`python -c \"import X; print(X.__version__)\"`)\n   - Surface-area sample call against ONE representative input (e.g., 1 ticker × 5 days for a market-data adapter)\n   - Capture exit code + stderr + first 200 chars of stdout\n4. **Build probe matrix** — write `data/<track>-probe-<vendor>.json` with one row per strategy:\n   ```json\n   {\"strategy\": \"A2\", \"verdict\": \"REJECT\", \"evidence\": \"vnstock.Quote returns VCI-only post-4.0.2; TCBS/DNSE/SSI/FMARKET deprecated\", \"probed_at\": \"2026-04-30T14:22:00+07:00\"}\n   ```\n5. **Pick** — first strategy with verdict=PASS; if multiple PASS, prefer simplest. If all REJECT → escalate to SCOPE-tier user-gate (do not silently degrade).\n6. **Document deviation** — if pick differs from master-plan recommendation, author IMPL-tier ADR (`agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-...md`) with `source_evidence` citing the probe matrix path. State explicitly: \"master-plan recommended X; empirical probe rejected X; adopted Y per probe matrix\".\n7. **Update lesson log** — if probe revealed stale source-evidence, append to `agent-notes.md` under \"Vendor API drift family (L-S28-1 / L-S32-1)\" cluster.\n\n## Outputs\n\n| Artifact | Path | Required? |\n|---|---|---|\n| Probe matrix (machine-readable) | `data/<track>-probe-<vendor>.json` | Yes |\n| Coverage report (human-readable) | `data/<track>-coverage-S{N}.md` | Yes if ladder picked involves vendor |\n| IMPL-tier ADR | `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-...md` | Yes if pick differs from recommendation |\n| SCOPE-tier escalation | AskUserQuestion (charter-tier split rule applies) | Only if all options REJECT |\n\n## Pre-Flight Checklist (binding when applies)\n\n- [ ] Active session-plan path resolved via `current-execution.md` (not hardcoded from memory)\n- [ ] Strategy enumeration is verbatim from source — no agent paraphrase\n- [ ] Each probe captured stderr (not just stdout) — silent failures are common with vendor SDKs\n- [ ] Probe matrix written BEFORE pick decision — order matters for audit trail\n- [ ] If pick differs from recommendation, IMPL-tier ADR cites probe matrix path in `source_evidence`\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n| Anti-pattern | Why bad | Correction |\n|---|---|---|\n| Trust the master-plan recommendation without probe | Vendor APIs drift silently; predecessor session's evidence may be stale | Always probe when ladder ≥3 |\n| Probe only the recommended option | Defeats the purpose; can't compare | Probe ALL viable options before pick |\n| Probe but pick recommendation anyway \"to honor the plan\" | Calibration drift; loses the empirical signal | Document the deviation as IMPL-tier ADR |\n| Silently degrade to last-viable when all REJECT | Hides scope reduction from human; violates UP-06 NO-Silent-Default | Escalate via AskUserQuestion SCOPE-tier |\n| Probe matrix fields opaque | Future audit cannot reconstruct decision | Use `{strategy, verdict, evidence, probed_at}` schema |\n\n## Pairs With\n\n- `scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh` — deterministic SessionStart hook detects ladder + emits advisory\n- `decompose-work` skill — splits each probe into deterministic (importability check) vs LLM (verdict synthesis)\n- `try-n-approaches` skill — Karpathy outer-loop framing; this skill is the strategy-pick lens within an approach\n- `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/012-track-A-source-pivot.md` — exemplar ADR following this protocol (S32 origin)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/013-S35-meta-loop-recovery-promote-routing.md` — S35 promote-routing decision that surfaced this skill as the next-tier promotion target\n\n## Provenance\n\n- **Origin**: L-S32-1 (\"Empirical probe before strategy commit\"; surfaced 2026-04-30 at S32 Track A R2 closure)\n- **Hook companion shipped**: S35 META_LOOP recovery (`scripts/hooks/vendor-api-probe.sh`)\n- **Skill promotion** (this file): Phase 2 close ceremony S43e (continuation 3); Phase 3 prereq #3 from `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/2026-05-04-phase-2-retrospective.md`\n- **Per Q-E3 promotion priority**: hook FIRST (DONE S35) → skill SECOND (THIS) → charter LAST (deferred unless ≥3 violations recur Phase 3)\n- **Cluster siblings**: L-S28-1 vendor-API drift family (vnstock 4.0.2 surface change); extends to KOL/news scrapers in Phase 3 (BeautifulSoup selector drift, RSS schema drift, etc.)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a2183e0a-a5a5-44f6-9a9c-5088003a779a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"a1bd31eb-d83b-46c7-9174-9e56af5946f5","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. {"parentUuid":"8dadef5b-9290-4d54-b79d-407d50054ee5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0f113386-2d0d-42ba-8b6d-aeb67bfc8ad9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Mw5SHTju6rGo2N6yTzr5Ny","type":"tool_result","content":"### 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE resume — HR-3 deploy): Bash printf With Format Starting `-` Needs `--` Sentinel\n**Context**: Authoring `scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh`. Lines like `printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"` failed with `printf: - : invalid option` because the format string starts with `-` and bash's printf interprets the first arg as a flag candidate. Same trap applied to `printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\"` when DISTINCT_FILES content lines start with `  - ...`. Two distinct sub-cases: (a) literal format-string starting with dash; (b) content-line starting with dash being passed through `%s`. Both fixed by `printf -- '...' ...` to terminate option parsing.\n**Rule**: When authoring deterministic Stop hooks (or any bash script writing markdown bullets), default-add `--` to every printf where format starts with `-` OR substituted content might. Cheaper than triaging a \"set -uo pipefail\" partial-write later. Test smoke immediately after Write.\n**Anti-example**: Trust `set -uo pipefail` + `trap 'exit 0' ERR` to catch printf failures gracefully — the trap fires AFTER the partial-write block aborted, so the rollup file is half-written and idempotency check next-run reads a corrupted output. (Encountered: rollup ended at \"## Summary\" with no body on first smoke; subsequent runs would have skipped because mtime newer than raw log.)\n**Correct example (this turn)**: After two iterative `--` patches caught by repeated smoke + `cat`, all four affected printf lines fixed. Final rollup ran clean: 621 entries / 26 files / structured H/M/L breakdown.\n**Severity**: medium (substrate; failed-silent partial-write would have broken HR-3 idempotency).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — `bash-hook-lint.sh` could grep for `printf '-` or `printf \"-` (no `--` separator) in `scripts/hooks/*.sh`. Promotion target: extend `bash-hook-lint.sh`. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-9.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE resume — premature-stop diagnosis): Harness Recovery DoD Must Be Explicit, Tidy-Summary Is Stop-Signal Anti-Pattern\n**Context**: Across 3 consecutive turns post-/clear (resume-verify → HR-3 deploy → HR-7 deploy), agent ended each turn with tidy 1-2 sentence summary signaling \"done\" while `current-execution.md` still listed HR-3/4/6/7 PENDING. User had to prompt \"continue\" → \"sao lại dừng lại rồi?\" → \"vấn đề là harness, chưa fix hết\". Agent was conflating immediate task-list completion with full charter-tier recovery completion. Memory rule `stop_offering_routing_branches` was misread as \"stop after each sub-task\" when its actual intent is \"don't enumerate (a)/(b)/(c) end-of-turn options; pick + execute\".\n**Rule**: When user pivots to CHARTER-TIER recovery (verbatim trigger phrase like \"fix toàn diện\" / \"comprehensive fix\"), agent MUST treat it as an open multi-turn arc, not a single task-list. Stop is permitted ONLY when ALL of:\n  (a) every HR-N item in `current-execution.md` recovery row has terminal status (✅ DEPLOYED / 🔒 GATED-HUMAN-APPROVAL / 🔭 PHASE-N-DEFERRED with explicit defer reason);\n  (b) no further deterministic work is unblocked (charter-amendment-draft / hook-deploy / smoke-test);\n  (c) external $ burn or human-gate is the only remaining unblock.\nEnd-of-turn summary MUST list remaining DoD items by name + status, NEVER tidy \"awaits next prompt\" while PENDING items exist.\n**Anti-example**: After deploying HR-3, write \"5 deterministic Stop hooks live; new lesson L-S43b-9; staged production awaits your commit\" — reads as DONE; HR-4/6/7 hidden in current-execution.md state. End-of-turn shows DoD checklist, not summary.\n**Severity**: critical (architectural; the meta-meta-loop failure user explicitly called out — premature-stop is itself a self-upgrade-loop dormancy mode).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — NEW deterministic Stop hook `harness-recovery-dod-watchdog.sh`: parses `current-execution.md` for active recovery row; greps for `⏳ PENDING` markers within HR-* items; if ≥1 PENDING + agent's last message text was tidy-summary-shaped (heuristic: <300 chars, ends with period, no DoD checklist) → exit 2 in strict mode OR ALERT in default. Promotion: ship same turn as part of THIS lesson's L-S43b-10 candidate.\n**Lesson candidate ID**: L-S43b-10. **HIGHEST PRIORITY** for promotion — this is the user-detected meta-failure that triggered \"fix toàn diện\" pivot itself; recurrence = trust loss.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b arc cross-session synthesis): Ghost-Work Pre-Flight Discovery Requires Explicit Provenance Audit\n**Context**: S43b-EVIDENCE VBW pre-flight found `packages/domain/market_data/services/ta_service.py` (164 LOC, 15 tests) already authored but untracked in git — no session log reference, no checkpoint entry. Agent adopted it immediately into the current session without tracing which session authored it, whether tests matched current spec, or why it was never staged.\n**Rule**: When VBW pre-flight discovers an untracked authored source file (non-test, non-config, non-generated): (1) `git log --all --full-history -- <path>` to check prior commit history; (2) Read the file fully; (3) Run its tests standalone; (4) Write a \"GHOST-WORK FOUND\" entry in the current session log with path, LOC count, test count, and session-of-origin if determinable; (5) Stage only after tests pass. Do NOT silently treat ghost-work as spec-aligned without the provenance audit.\n**Anti-example**: Pre-flight reports \"ta_service.py exists (untracked); was untracked → now tracked.\" — no provenance trace, no session log \"GHOST-WORK FOUND\", file silently adopted into scope as if it were current-session work.\n**Correct example**: Pre-flight discovers `ta_service.py`; agent notes \"GHOST-WORK: no session log reference found; authored in unknown prior partial session; read + 15 tests run standalone → all pass → staging with archaeological note\"; session log records the find explicitly.\n**Severity**: medium (data-integrity risk: ghost-work may be stale, may not match current spec, may have been abandoned for a reason — silent adoption skips all these checks).\n**Auto-detect**: partial — Stop hook could grep `git status --short` output for `?? packages/` (untracked source files) and cross-check session log for \"GHOST-WORK FOUND\" entry; ALERT if untracked source files adopted silently. {"parentUuid":"50284535-bca1-4182-b7d6-301d9593d493","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"1fff03e3-bc05-42da-8fe3-17ece46a8111","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EEtW57d9QUjeinrYdpYrSJ","type":"tool_result","content":"120\t- **Redis** (cache + pub/sub + queue broker)\n121\t- **Cloudflare R2** (S3-compatible, raw content storage)\n122\t- **DuckDB** (ad-hoc analytics, backtesting fast scans)\n123\t\n124\t### LLM\n125\t\n126\t- **Primary**: Claude API\n127\t  - Sonnet 4.6+ for routine extraction, classification\n128\t  - Opus 4.7+ for thesis synthesis, multi-perspective adversarial\n129\t- **Embeddings**: OpenAI text-embedding-3-small (works for Vietnamese)\n130\t- **Whisper**: for YouTube/podcast transcription (local or OpenAI API)\n131\t- **OSS fallback (Phase 4+)**: Llama 3.3 70B via Ollama for cost reduction\n132\t\n133\t### Frontend\n134\t\n135\t- **Phase 1-2**: Streamlit (rapid personal dashboard)\n136\t- **Phase 3+**: Next.js 14 with shadcn/ui (only if SaaS path activated)\n137\t- Always include: TradingView lightweight charts integration\n138\t\n139\t### Workers\n140\t\n141\t- **Python workers**: Dramatiq (async tasks), Prefect (scheduled DAGs)\n142\t- **Schedule examples**:\n143\t  - Hourly: news ingestion, sentiment refresh\n144\t  - Daily 16:00: market data EOD update, screener run\n145\t  - Daily 18:00: KOL channel scan\n146\t  - Weekly Sun: pattern recalibration\n147\t  - Monthly: thesis post-mortem batch\n148\t\n149\t### Infrastructure\n150\t\n151\t- **Monorepo**: pnpm workspaces (even Python apps benefit from it for shared tooling), or Hatch + uv if going pure Python\n152\t- **Deploy (early)**: Single VPS (Hetzner) — personal use\n153\t- **Deploy (scale, if needed)**: Modal/Fly.io for workers, Hetzner dedicated for DB\n154\t- **Observability**: Sentry + simple Grafana for metrics\n155\t\n156\t---\n157\t\n158\t## Folder Conventions\n159\t\n160\t### Per Bounded Context\n161\t\n162\t```\n163\tpackages/domain/<bc_name>/\n164\t├── models/           # Entity classes (rich, with behavior)"}]},"uuid":"d33b74a1-4035-4677-912f-6b07ed47f855","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:16:30.072Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\constitution\\architecture.md","content":"- **Redis** (cache + pub/sub + queue broker)\n- **Cloudflare R2** (S3-compatible, raw content storage)\n- **DuckDB** (ad-hoc analytics, backtesting fast scans)\n\n### LLM\n\n- **Primary**: Claude API\n  - Sonnet 4.6+ for routine extraction, classification\n  - Opus 4.7+ for thesis synthesis, multi-perspective adversarial\n- **Embeddings**: OpenAI text-embedding-3-small (works for Vietnamese)\n- **Whisper**: for YouTube/podcast transcription (local or OpenAI API)\n- **OSS fallback (Phase 4+)**: Llama 3.3 70B via Ollama for cost reduction\n\n### Frontend\n\n- **Phase 1-2**: Streamlit (rapid personal dashboard)\n- **Phase 3+**: Next.js 14 with shadcn/ui (only if SaaS path activated)\n- Always include: TradingView lightweight charts integration\n\n### Workers\n\n- **Python workers**: Dramatiq (async tasks), Prefect (scheduled DAGs)\n- **Schedule examples**:\n  - Hourly: news ingestion, sentiment refresh\n  - Daily 16:00: market data EOD update, screener run\n  - Daily 18:00: KOL channel scan\n  - Weekly Sun: pattern recalibration\n  - Monthly: thesis post-mortem batch\n\n### Infrastructure\n\n- **Monorepo**: pnpm workspaces (even Python apps benefit from it for shared tooling), or Hatch + uv if going pure Python\n- **Deploy (early)**: Single VPS (Hetzner) — personal use\n- **Deploy (scale, if needed)**: Modal/Fly.io for workers, Hetzner dedicated for DB\n- **Observability**: Sentry + simple Grafana for metrics\n\n---\n\n## Folder Conventions\n\n### Per Bounded Context\n\n```\npackages/domain/<bc_name>/\n├── models/           # Entity classes (rich, with behavior)","numLines":45,"startLine":120,"totalLines":433}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"50284535-bca1-4182-b7d6-301d9593d493","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b2c74573-639c-411d-992c-b08bb3234172","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01MoLUYd5pMiqYrjxJaBm26r","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012M6M5BkoLbFGdebfkUG4xR","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\proposals\\architecture-amendment-C1-llm-substrate-boundary.md","content":"---\nproposal_id: architecture-amendment-C1-llm-substrate-boundary\ntitle: Architecture amendment C1 — append \"LLM substrate boundary\" cross-ref section to architecture.md\nstatus: PROPOSAL\ndraft_date: 2026-05-04\ndraft_session: S43e (continuation 4)\nauthoring_agent: Claude Opus 4.7\ntarget_charter_file: agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md\ngating: USER-EXPLICIT-PICK-REQUIRED (charter-edit deny-lift cycle per S38 mechanism)\nprovenance:\n  promote_rule_observation: agent-workspace/memory/observations/promote-rule-S43c.md § Cluster C1 + § Routing Plan row 4\n  source_rules:\n    - KI-S43b-1  # Bull sonnet prompt pairing reproduces 300s subprocess timeout\n    - KI-S43b-2  # LLM JSON wrapped in prose preamble breaks naive json.loads\n    - KI-S43b-3  # Windows cp1252 default encoding crashes claude CLI subprocess\n    - BP-S43b-1  # Per-role model override pattern for LLM-perspective adapters\n    - BP-S43b-2  # Prose-tolerant JSON extractor for LLM structured output\n    - BP-S43b-3  # Wire deterministic compute services through gatherer, not agent\n  promotion_priority: charter (per Q-E3 hook FIRST → skill SECOND → charter LAST; this cluster's hook+code mitigations already shipped at S43b; charter cross-ref makes the canonical reference durable)\n  related_existing_artifacts:\n    - packages/infrastructure/analysis/claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py  # role_model_overrides field (BP-S43b-1)\n    - packages/infrastructure/analysis/subagent_transport.py:55-118       # 3-tier JSON extractor (BP-S43b-2)\n    - apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py::_build_subagent_agents             # gatherer-wired compute (BP-S43b-3)\n---\n\n# Proposal: Architecture amendment C1 — LLM substrate boundary cross-ref\n\n> **Status**: DRAFT — proposed for charter ratification at user discretion.\n> **Tier**: CHARTER (architecture.md is constitution-tier; edit requires deny-lift cycle per S38 mechanism — same channel that ratified D-015..D-024 Phase 2 charter promotes).\n> **Required action**: User explicit letter pick per Q-B2 closure (charter-tier MUST require explicit accept; default-acceptance prohibited).\n> **Bundling opportunity**: Pair with C2 `proposals/decision-discipline-amendment-rule-4b.md` for a single-batch deny-lift cycle covering both Phase 2 close charter promotes.\n\n## Why this amendment exists\n\nS43b harness recovery (sandwich-dev + verifier across 5 sub-sessions) surfaced 3 known-issues + 3 best-practices specifically about the LLM substrate seam — the boundary where deterministic Python code meets the non-deterministic LLM call. All three best-practices have already shipped as production code (citations in frontmatter). What is missing is the **canonical pointer**: when a future BC adds an LLM-perspective adapter (BC-8 already shipped; future BCs may add their own — KOL summarization, news classification, pump narrative detection), where does the agent look to find these patterns?\n\nWithout a charter cross-ref, the patterns live only in `self-awareness/best-practices.md` (a long, append-only file) and in the production code itself. Discovery is grep-dependent, not architecture-document-driven.\n\n## Proposed amendment text\n\nAppend a new subsection to `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` immediately after § \"LLM\" (currently lines 124-131, between \"OSS fallback\" line and \"### Frontend\" line):\n\n```markdown\n### LLM Substrate Boundary\n\nThe boundary where deterministic Python code calls into a non-deterministic LLM\nis a **failure-prone seam** — Phase 2 (S43b) surfaced 3 distinct failure modes\nand shipped 3 mitigation patterns. Future LLM-perspective adapters (BC-8 today;\nany BC tomorrow that adds LLM extraction or classification) MUST adopt these\npatterns:\n\n- **Per-role model override** (BP-S43b-1): adapters expose `role_model_overrides`\n  so cost/latency-sensitive roles (e.g., Bull perspective) can downgrade Opus → Haiku\n  without touching call sites. Reference: `claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py`.\n- **Prose-tolerant JSON extractor** (BP-S43b-2): never call naive `json.loads`\n  on LLM output; use the 3-tier extractor (fenced block → first-`{` heuristic →\n  best-effort recovery). Reference: `subagent_transport.py:55-118`.\n- **Gatherer-wired deterministic compute** (BP-S43b-3): numbers come from code\n  (Charter Principle 9 / I-S1) — wire deterministic services (RatioService,\n  TaService, etc.) through the gatherer that prepares LLM context, not through\n  the LLM agent itself.\n\nKnown failure modes catalogued: KI-S43b-1 (sonnet pairing 300s subprocess timeout),\nKI-S43b-2 (LLM JSON in prose preamble), KI-S43b-3 (Windows cp1252 encoding crash).\nSee `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/{known-issues,best-practices}.md`.\n```\n\nTotal: ~22 LOC including header + bullets + references. Promote-rule estimate of \"~6 LOC\" understated the cite density required for the cross-ref to be navigable; this draft errs on the side of completeness.\n\n## Why charter-tier (not skill, not just hook)\n\nPer Q-E3 promotion priority — hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST. This cluster lands at charter because:\n\n1. **Hook tier already DONE implicitly**: the 3 mitigations are baked into production code paths; no separate hook needed because the patterns ARE the code.\n2. **Skill tier inappropriate**: this is not a procedure (no LLM-judgment-required steps); it's a structural reference for \"where do I look when adding an LLM-perspective adapter\". That's architecture-document territory.\n3. **Charter cross-ref is the durable form**: future-Claude reading architecture.md gets the pointer; future-Claude reading only `best-practices.md` may not realize these patterns are *required* for new LLM-perspective adapters, treating them as optional advice instead.\n\n## Trade-offs\n\n| Concern | Mitigation |\n|---|---|\n| **architecture.md grows over time as more substrate boundaries get cross-refs** | Acceptable; architecture.md is the canonical structural reference and IS supposed to enumerate substrate boundaries. The grow-rate is bounded by number of distinct failure-mode clusters surfaced (Phase 2 = 1 cluster; expect ~1 per phase). |\n| **Cross-ref text could go stale if BP-S43b-* IDs renumber** | Renumbering is forbidden by self-awareness append-only contract. IDs are stable for life of the project. |\n| **Future BCs may add patterns the cross-ref doesn't cover** | Each future cluster gets its own append at the same § \"LLM Substrate Boundary\" location; no rewrite of existing cite. |\n| **Charter-edit ceremony cost (deny-lift cycle)** | Bundle with C2 (Rule 4b) per \"Bundling opportunity\" above; 1 deny-lift cycle for both. |\n\n## Failure modes if NOT ratified\n\n- **Pattern rot**: future LLM-perspective adapters re-derive (or worse, omit) per-role override, prose-tolerant extractor, gatherer-wired compute. Each omission re-creates a KI-S43b-* class incident.\n- **Documentation fragmentation**: best-practices.md becomes the only source-of-truth pointer; agents consulting architecture.md get a wrong picture (LLM section says \"use Claude API\" but doesn't warn about the substrate failure modes).\n\n## What ratification looks like (for the user)\n\nIf the user picks \"ACCEPT\":\n1. Agent moves the amendment text from this proposal to `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` via the S38 deny-lift mechanism (charter-edit gate).\n2. Insertion point: between current line 131 (\"OSS fallback (Phase 4+): Llama 3.3 70B via Ollama for cost reduction\") and current line 133 (\"### Frontend\").\n3. Agent authors `D-NNN-S43e-charter-promote-architecture-llm-substrate-boundary.md` ADR with approval_chain pointing to the user pick.\n\nIf the user picks \"REJECT\":\n1. Mark this proposal `status: REJECTED` with reason.\n2. Patterns continue to live only in `best-practices.md`; agent-notes.md retains the cluster.\n\nIf the user picks \"AMEND\":\n1. User specifies which clause to change (most likely candidate: shorten the amendment back toward the original \"~6 LOC\" target).\n2. Agent revises this proposal; re-presents.\n\n## Related open proposals (sibling-cluster context)\n\n- `proposals/decision-discipline-amendment-rule-4b.md` (C2 — drafted S43e; same deny-lift batch candidate).\n- 7 Phase 2 charter promotes already shipped via deny-lift (D-015..D-024); same channel applies here.\n\n## Ratification record\n\n(Empty until user pick lands.)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2166,"cache_read_input_tokens":149384,"output_tokens":3627,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2166,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3627,"cache_read_input_tokens":149384,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2166,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2166},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":{"cache_miss_reason":{"type":"unavailable"}}},"requestId":"req_011CahkVfuHYchPcjyDFcpTm","type":"assistant","uuid":"c366a25d-a8ba-4526-a625-aa3dd97301cc","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:17:34.196Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Caller (Phase1DataGatherer) reads `gaps` separately.\n        \"\"\"\n        out: dict[RatioName, Ratio] = {}\n        ttm_is = self._aggregate_ttm(income_statements)\n        net_income_ttm = ttm_is.get(LineItemKey.NET_INCOME.value)\n        revenue_ttm = ttm_is.get(LineItemKey.REVENUE.value)\n\n        if revenue_ttm is not None and net_income_ttm is not None:\n            with contextlib.suppress(RatioComputationError):\n                out[RatioName.NET_MARGIN] = self.compute_net_margin(\n                    net_income_ttm=net_income_ttm, revenue_ttm=revenue_ttm,\n                    ticker=ticker, period_end=period_end,\n                )\n\n        if latest_balance_sheet is None:\n            return out\n\n        equity = latest_balance_sheet.get_line_item(LineItemKey.TOTAL_EQUITY)\n        assets = latest_balance_sheet.get_line_item(LineItemKey.TOTAL_ASSETS)\n        liab = latest_balance_sheet.get_line_item(LineItemKey.TOTAL_LIABILITIES)\n        bvps = latest_balance_sheet.get_line_item(LineItemKey.BOOK_VALUE_PER_SHARE)\n\n        if net_income_ttm is not None and equity is not None:\n            with contextlib.suppress(RatioComputationError):\n                out[RatioName.ROE] = self.compute_roe(","numLines":35,"startLine":145,"totalLines":256}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fbc74ff7-3888-478c-b45f-6bb12e408bfb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"29e85a82-8cba-4462-877e-002144b766e4","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Lm7LsUozbfj4sQBfyLm4XM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ABX63QVMmsBWjypzKagaGV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\observations\\defer-s43b-status-S43e.md","content":"---\nobservation_id: defer-s43b-status-S43e\ntype: status-assessment\ncreated_at: 2026-05-04\nrelated_session: S43e (continuation 5)\npurpose: clarify current state of DEFER-S43b-1 (cost ledger drift) + DEFER-S43b-2 (RatioService bank schema Q-S28-3) at Phase 2 close, so Phase 3 entry session does not re-investigate stale defers\n---\n\n# DEFER-S43b-1 / DEFER-S43b-2 status assessment\n\n## DEFER-S43b-1 — cost ledger drift verification → ✅ EMPIRICALLY RESOLVED (recommend close)\n\n**Original concern** (S43b harness recovery): cost ledger was reporting `$0` because the LLM adapter error-path swallowed cost data when JSON parse failed. Fixed at S43b via 3-tier prose-tolerant JSON extractor (`subagent_transport.py:55-118`) which prevents the error-path from being entered for typical prose-wrapped LLM output.\n\n**Empirical evidence post-fix**:\n- S43b last turn: `$0.95 bubbled correctly, no longer $0 from error path` (per `current-execution.md` S43b-EVIDENCE row line 189)\n- S43d 5-thesis dogfood (BID/BVH/CTG/FPT/GAS): 5/5 reported real cost figures totalling $6.8413 (avg $1.37 per thesis)\n- S43e VF-3 CTG reproducibility: $0.8178 reported (vs S43d baseline $0.9099 — both real, both within VF-2 ≤$2 average target)\n\n**Source-of-truth code path verified intact** (this observation, not new investigation):\n- `claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py:47` documents subagent transport CLI-reported `total_cost_usd` as authoritative\n- `perspectives/{bear,bull,quant}_agent.py` all unpack `(raw_json, cost_usd, model_id, prompt_hash)` from `call_llm()` and pass `cost_usd=Decimal(str(cost_usd))` to perspective record (preserves precision per Charter Principle 9 / I-S1)\n- 0 hits for `cost_usd = 0` or `cost_usd = Decimal(0)` in error-path code\n\n**Recommendation**: close DEFER-S43b-1 at next checkpoint write. The \"drift verification\" is concretely satisfied by 6 separate dogfood runs (S43b-EVIDENCE-FPT + S43d × 5 + S43e × 1) all reporting real cost figures. No further investigation needed unless cost reverts to $0 in Phase 3 dogfood.\n\n## DEFER-S43b-2 — RatioService bank schema (Q-S28-3) → 🔭 GENUINELY OPEN (Phase 3 sizing decision)\n\n**Original concern** (S28 BC-2 Fundamental authoring): bank tickers (BID, CTG, VCB, MBB, etc. — ~7 of VN30) use a different chart-of-accounts than non-bank tickers (CAR / NIM / NPL / LDR / CIR are the standard bank ratios; P/E + P/B still apply but ROE / D/E / Net Margin / ROA need bank-specific formulas using net interest income, provisions, etc.).\n\n**Q-S28-3 doctrine adopted at S28**: skip silently per \"log + continue\" — the `RatioService.compute_phase2_ratios` method returns only ratios where all required inputs exist; missing inputs are NOT errors, just absent rows in the output dict (`ratio_service.py:154` doc comment).\n\n**Empirical Phase 2 effect** (per S43d 5-thesis dogfood):\n- 5 tickers selected: BID (bank), BVH (insurance), CTG (bank), FPT (tech), GAS (oil & gas)\n- BID + CTG = 2/5 bank tickers\n- Bear/Bull/Quant agents handled bank-ticker fundamental gaps via Rule-7 honest-insufficient narrative (\"limited fundamentals; skip ratio-based VALUE dimension\")\n- VF-2 cost target met ($6.84 / $1.37 avg ≤ $2 spec); thesis_id deterministic across re-runs (VF-3 PASS)\n- Real harm signal: 0 (no thesis incorrectly synthesized; all flagged data gaps explicitly per Rule-7)\n\n**3 options for Phase 3 sizing**:\n\n- **Option A — Keep doctrine** (RECOMMENDED Phase 3 entry): the \"log + continue\" doctrine empirically works; bank-thesis quality limited by data-gap-attribution narrative, not by missing ratios. Ship Phase 3 with bank-as-special-case-via-narrative; revisit only if peer-comparable spec § A.10 gates on bank-specific ratios.\n- **Option B — Implement bank-schema ratios**: extend RatioService with `compute_bank_ratios()` returning CAR/NIM/NPL/LDR/CIR; requires BC-2 ingestion to also fetch bank-specific line-items (deposit liabilities, loan portfolio breakdown, NPL classification). Estimated 1 sub-session ~80-120K (FOCUSED_IMPL); would close Q-S28-3 but adds ingestion-side complexity.\n- **Option C — SCOPE-tier defer**: explicitly defer bank-schema to Phase 4+ with charter-tier note (bank fundamental analysis is its own discipline; treating as Phase 3 scope dilutes Phase 3 KOL/pump focus).\n\n**Recommendation**: Option A (keep doctrine) for Phase 3 entry; revisit only if Phase 3 dogfood reveals bank-thesis quality is materially worse than non-bank thesis quality. Phase 3 master-plan should explicitly list this as a deferred line-item, not a hidden gap.\n\n## Action items for Phase 3 entry session\n\n1. Fixed at S43b via 3-tier prose-tolerant JSON extractor (`subagent_transport.py:55-118`) which prevents the error-path from being entered for typical prose-wrapped LLM output.\n\n**Empirical evidence post-fix**:\n- S43b last turn: `$0.95 bubbled correctly, no longer $0 from error path` (per `current-execution.md` S43b-EVIDENCE row line 189)\n- S43d 5-thesis dogfood (BID/BVH/CTG/FPT/GAS): 5/5 reported real cost figures totalling $6.8413 (avg $1.37 per thesis)\n- S43e VF-3 CTG reproducibility: $0.8178 reported (vs S43d baseline $0.9099 — both real, both within VF-2 ≤$2 average target)\n\n**Source-of-truth code path verified intact** (this observation, not new investigation):\n- `claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py:47` documents subagent transport CLI-reported `total_cost_usd` as authoritative\n- `perspectives/{bear,bull,quant}_agent.py` all unpack `(raw_json, cost_usd, model_id, prompt_hash)` from `call_llm()` and pass `cost_usd=Decimal(str(cost_usd))` to perspective record (preserves precision per Charter Principle 9 / I-S1)\n- 0 hits for `cost_usd = 0` or `cost_usd = Decimal(0)` in error-path code\n\n**Recommendation**: close DEFER-S43b-1 at next checkpoint write. The \"drift verification\" is concretely satisfied by 6 separate dogfood runs (S43b-EVIDENCE-FPT + S43d × 5 + S43e × 1) all reporting real cost figures. No further investigation needed unless cost reverts to $0 in Phase 3 dogfood.\n\n## DEFER-S43b-2 — RatioService bank schema (Q-S28-3) → 🔭 GENUINELY OPEN (Phase 3 sizing decision)\n\n**Original concern** (S28 BC-2 Fundamental authoring): bank tickers (BID, CTG, VCB, MBB, etc. — ~7 of VN30) use a different chart-of-accounts than non-bank tickers (CAR / NIM / NPL / LDR / CIR are the standard bank ratios; P/E + P/B still apply but ROE / D/E / Net Margin / ROA need bank-specific formulas using net interest income, provisions, etc.).\n\n**Q-S28-3 doctrine adopted at S28**: skip silently per \"log + continue\" — the `RatioService.compute_phase2_ratios` method returns only ratios where all required inputs exist; missing inputs are NOT errors, just absent rows in the output dict (`ratio_service.py:154` doc comment).\n\n**Empirical Phase 2 effect** (per S43d 5-thesis dogfood):\n- 5 tickers selected: BID (bank), BVH (insurance), CTG (bank), FPT (tech), GAS (oil & gas)\n- BID + CTG = 2/5 bank tickers\n- Bear/Bull/Quant agents handled bank-ticker fundamental gaps via Rule-7 honest-insufficient narrative (\"limited fundamentals; skip ratio-based VALUE dimension\")\n- VF-2 cost target met ($6.84 / $1.37 avg ≤ $2 spec); thesis_id deterministic across re-runs (VF-3 PASS)\n- Real harm signal: 0 (no thesis incorrectly synthesized; all flagged data gaps explicitly per Rule-7)\n\n**3 options for Phase 3 sizing**:\n\n- **Option A — Keep doctrine** (RECOMMENDED Phase 3 entry): the \"log + continue\" doctrine empirically works; bank-thesis quality limited by data-gap-attribution narrative, not by missing ratios. Ship Phase 3 with bank-as-special-case-via-narrative; revisit only if peer-comparable spec § A.10 gates on bank-specific ratios.\n- **Option B — Implement bank-schema ratios**: extend RatioService with `compute_bank_ratios()` returning CAR/NIM/NPL/LDR/CIR; requires BC-2 ingestion to also fetch bank-specific line-items (deposit liabilities, loan portfolio breakdown, NPL classification). Estimated 1 sub-session ~80-120K (FOCUSED_IMPL); would close Q-S28-3 but adds ingestion-side complexity.\n- **Option C — SCOPE-tier defer**: explicitly defer bank-schema to Phase 4+ with charter-tier note (bank fundamental analysis is its own discipline; treating as Phase 3 scope dilutes Phase 3 KOL/pump focus).\n\n**Recommendation**: Option A (keep doctrine) for Phase 3 entry; revisit only if Phase 3 dogfood reveals bank-thesis quality is materially worse than non-bank thesis quality. {"parentUuid":"e00bcb8e-b989-40c8-ba84-a2c0ef66f7ba","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"888cf336-e1d0-4ab6-9840-29d8329ceb7f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NLx1Ax2qKk1yWnRxketoAE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# ghost-work-audit.sh — SessionStart hook (C4 promote-rule S43c).\n3\t#\n4\t# Detects ghost-work: untracked source files under packages/ that were authored\n5\t# in a prior partial session but never staged, never logged in any session log,\n6\t# never referenced in checkpoints. Per L-S43b-11 (KI-S43b-8 / agent-notes\n7\t# 2026-05-01 § \"Ghost-Work Pre-Flight Discovery Requires Explicit Provenance\n8\t# Audit\"): silent adoption of ghost-work is a data-integrity risk; surface to\n9\t# the agent at SessionStart so VBW pre-flight covers provenance.\n10\t#\n11\t# Exempt: tests (test_*.py, *_test.py), __init__.py, configs, generated files.\n12\t# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Soft-warn (exit 0); never blocks.\n13\t\n14\tset -uo pipefail\n15\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n16\t\n17\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n18\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n19\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n20\t\n21\tcd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\n22\tcommand -v git >/dev/null 2>&1 || exit 0\n23\t\n24\tUNTRACKED=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null | awk '$1==\"??\"{print $2}' \\\n25\t  | grep -E '^packages/.+\\.py$' \\\n26\t  | grep -vE '(test_|_test\\.py$|__init__\\.py$|/migrations/|/generated/)' \\\n27\t  || true)\"\n28\t\n29\t[ -z \"$UNTRACKED\" ] && {\n30\t  printf '[%s] ghost-work-audit OK (0 untracked source files under packages/)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\n31\t  exit 0\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\tCOUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$UNTRACKED\" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\"\n35\tLATEST_SESSION=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null \\\n36\t  | xargs -I{} stat -c '%Y {}' {} 2>/dev/null | sort -rn | head -1 | awk '{print $2}' || true)\"\n37\t\n38\tDOCUMENTED=0\n39\t[ -n \"$LATEST_SESSION\" ] && grep -qiE 'GHOST-WORK FOUND|ghost.work.found' \"$LATEST_SESSION\" 2>/dev/null && DOCUMENTED=1\n40\t\n41\tif [ \"$DOCUMENTED\" -eq 1 ]; then\n42\t  printf '[%s] ghost-work-audit INFO %d untracked source file(s) but latest session log documents GHOST-WORK FOUND\\n' \"$TS\" \"$COUNT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n43\t  exit 0\n44\tfi\n45\t\n46\tprintf '[%s] ghost-work-audit ALERT %d untracked source file(s) under packages/ — VBW provenance audit required (L-S43b-11):\\n' \"$TS\" \"$COUNT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n47\tprintf '%s\\n' \"$UNTRACKED\" | sed 's/^/  - /' >> \"$LOG\"\n48\techo \"ghost-work-audit ALERT: $COUNT untracked source file(s) under packages/ require provenance audit per L-S43b-11. Run \\`git log --all --full-history -- <path>\\` per file; add 'GHOST-WORK FOUND' entry to current session log before staging.\" >&2\n49\t\n50\texit 0\n51\t"}]},"uuid":"12fec43c-41f8-42c3-8477-ac96cf6df18f","timestamp":"2026-05-04T15:23:53.380Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\ghost-work-audit.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# ghost-work-audit.sh — SessionStart hook (C4 promote-rule S43c).\n#\n# Detects ghost-work: untracked source files under packages/ that were authored\n# in a prior partial session but never staged, never logged in any session log,\n# never referenced in checkpoints. Per L-S43b-11 (KI-S43b-8 / agent-notes\n# 2026-05-01 § \"Ghost-Work Pre-Flight Discovery Requires Explicit Provenance\n# Audit\"): silent adoption of ghost-work is a data-integrity risk; surface to\n# the agent at SessionStart so VBW pre-flight covers provenance.\n#\n# Exempt: tests (test_*.py, *_test.py), __init__.py, configs, generated files.\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Soft-warn (exit 0); never blocks.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\ncommand -v git >/dev/null 2>&1 || exit 0\n\nUNTRACKED=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null | awk '$1==\"??\"{print $2}' \\\n  | grep -E '^packages/.+\\.py$' \\\n  | grep -vE '(test_|_test\\.py$|__init__\\.py$|/migrations/|/generated/)' \\\n  || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$UNTRACKED\" ] && {\n  printf '[%s] ghost-work-audit OK (0 untracked source files under packages/)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\n}\n\nCOUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$UNTRACKED\" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\"\nLATEST_SESSION=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} stat -c '%Y {}' {} 2>/dev/null | sort -rn | head -1 | awk '{print $2}' || true)\"\n\nDOCUMENTED=0\n[ -n \"$LATEST_SESSION\" ] && grep -qiE 'GHOST-WORK FOUND|ghost.work.found' \"$LATEST_SESSION\" 2>/dev/null && DOCUMENTED=1\n\nif [ \"$DOCUMENTED\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] ghost-work-audit INFO %d untracked source file(s) but latest session log documents GHOST-WORK FOUND\\n' \"$TS\" \"$COUNT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nprintf '[%s] ghost-work-audit ALERT %d untracked source file(s) under packages/ — VBW provenance audit required (L-S43b-11):\\n' \"$TS\" \"$COUNT\" >> \"$LOG\"\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$UNTRACKED\" | sed 's/^/  - /' >> \"$LOG\"\necho \"ghost-work-audit ALERT: $COUNT untracked source file(s) under packages/ require provenance audit per L-S43b-11. Run \\`git log --all --full-history -- <path>\\` per file; add 'GHOST-WORK FOUND' entry to current session log before staging.\" >&2\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":51,"startLine":1,"totalLines":51}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e00bcb8e-b989-40c8-ba84-a2c0ef66f7ba","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"69023f84-9a0f-40c9-be6c-0e5915b30b2b","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
